Let's design binary local search in detail.

Function `make_binary(h, n)` as above.
Evaluate `c_counts = np.correlate(b, 1-b, mode='full')` (array int). Since b and 1-b arrays, result int. T=int(c.max()). dx=2.0/n. cval=T*dx.

Greedy/SA binary search:
- Ensure n even. Choose n=1000 or 2000? Larger n target_T larger; local search dimension larger. Start n=1000. We can upsample best h to 1000 before binarize.
- Maintain b (uint8), c counts int array length 2n-1. `get_max` T.
- Candidate generation:
   a. Random pairs: choose random one and zero indices.
   b. Active-based: For top shifts where c==T, identify positions with b_i=1 and b_j=0 contributing. We can sample one from a contributing 1 and one from a contributing 0 for each active shift; but the pair in same shift if swapped? Actually to reduce c at shift k, we want flip i=1->0 or j=0->1. The pair swap needs one such 1 (could be from some active shift) and one such 0 (could be from same or other). We can sample i among union of high-contributing ones, j among union zeros. This likely reduce.
   We can compute `contrib_ones` and `contrib_zeros` for top shifts: for shift offset k, pairs (i,j); ones positions i with b_i=1 and b_j=0; zeros positions j with b_i=1 and b_j=0. We just need positions keys. For each active shift, get arrays of ones prefix/suffix and zeros suffix/prefix; mask where b_other=0 / b_other=1. Use np.nonzero. Then sample up to some count. But doing this every iteration maybe O(active*n) okay.
- Evaluating swap: apply b[i]=0,b[j]=1; compute c_new=correlate; if max < T or (SA and accept), update c; else revert. Full correlate for each candidate expensive. To reduce, use delta arrays as described. Let's implement delta to make more candidates cheap.

Precompute flip delta rows? For n=1000, L=1999, rows length L. For each position p, value of flipping from 0 to1 as a vector can be computed once per state using b. But after swaps, rows for i,j change. Could recompute all rows each iteration? O(nL)=2e6 per iteration, too heavy. But we can compute delta for a pair on the fly with vectorized operations O(n) but maybe cheaper than full correlate? Let's derive on-the-fly delta for swap i (1->0) and j (0->1): delta = delta01_iposs? We can compute effect of toggling single bit p from val to 1-val on c. Since original c=correlate(b,1-b). Toggling p affects c at shifts where p participates. We can create delta array zeros and fill intervals:
Let flip p from 1 to0:
- p as first: for each q such that pair (p,q) within length. If q>p with k=q-p ≥0? In full correlate orientation, c[r] = ∑_{i} b_i (1-b_{i+k}) for k = r-(n-1)? Let's fix. Let's work with shift k (integer) from -(n-1) to n-1, where c(k)=∑_{i} b_i * (1-b_{i+k}) when 0≤i<n,0≤i+k<n. For k>0: i=0..n-k-1, second index i+k. For k<0: let d=-k, i=d..n-1, second i-d. Now toggle p 1→0:
   * Terms where p is first: c(k) for k = q-p where q in [0,n) and 0≤p<n, 0≤q<n. value removed = (1-b_q). So for each q, shift k=q-p, delta -= (1-b_q). That is all q; delta vector has entries for k in [-p, n-1-p] depending q.
   * Terms where p is second: c(k) where p = i+k ⇒ i=p-k. value c includes b_i*(1-b_p). Since b_p 1→0, (1-b_p) 0→1, add b_i for k = p-i where i∈[0,n). So for each i, shift k=p-i, delta += b_i. k range [p-n+1, p].
 For flip p 0→1:
   * first: add (1-b_q) for all q.
   * second: remove b_i for all i (because (1-b_p) 1→0, add? If b_p=0 to1, term b_i*(1-b_p) goes from b_i to0, delta -=b_i.)
Thus for swap i 1→0, j 0→1, delta = -A_i + B_i + A_j - B_j? Where A_p vector z with (1-b_q) at shift q-p (all q), B_p vector with b_i at shift p-i (all i). We can compute A_p and B_p quickly using sign? A_p is not just constant; values along q are 1-b_q placed at offsets. B_p similar b_i. We can precompute arrays of b and 1-b; to compute A_p delta for a single p, we can construct array of length L by shifting b. For candidate, doing this O(n) each (two flips =4 shifts) maybe 4000 ops, much less than full correlate 1e6? In Python loops with slicing maybe okay. We can compute delta = zeros(L); use index = p - (n-1) for center? Let's use correlate index r = n-1+k. For flip p, A contribution (first) for q: k=q-p, r=n-1+q-p. val=1-b[q]. So we can do `delta[n-1 + (q - p)] += sign*(1-b[q])` for q=0..n-1. This is a shifted copy of 1-b. For B: i: k=p-i, r=n-1+p-i, val=b[i]. So shifted reversed copy of b. We can vectorize with slices:
For A: positions r = n-1 - p + q, q=0..n-1, a contiguous slice from start = n-1-p to start+n-1 length n. Add sign*(1-b[q]) to delta[start:start+n].
For B: positions r = n-1 + p - i, i=0..n-1, decreasing. start = n-1 + p. For i=0..n-1, r decreases. Equivalent add sign*b to slice [start-n+1 : start+1] reversed: `delta[start-n+1:start+1] += sign*b[::-1]`.
Thus delta for flip p from 1 to0:
  delta[:]+= - (1-b) shifted A (since first remove)
  delta[:]+= + b shifted B (second add)
Flipping 0 to1:
  delta += (1-b) shifted A
  delta += - b shifted B
So for swap i 1→0, j 0→1:
  delta = -A_i + B_i + A_j - B_j, where A_p is vector with (1-b) shifted, B_p is vector with b shifted reversed.
We can compute A/B slices without creating arrays? We can zero delta L, then 4 slice additions. L~2000, 4*1000=4000 operations, very fast. Then newc = c + delta (vector) and max. This is maybe 100x faster than full correlate. Great! We can maintain c array exactly and update delta on accept.

Let's handle self pairs.

General delta for flipping p from old to new:
For all pairs (i,j) with i-j = k. Delta = new h_i(1-h_j)-old h_i(1-h_j). Cases:
- i=p, j≠p: old h_p=old, new=new; delta = (new-old)*(1-h_j).
- j=p, i≠p: old contribution h_i(1-old), new h_i(1-new); delta = h_i*(old-new).
- i=j=p: term always 0, delta0.
If old=1,new=0: (new-old)=-1, old-new=-1. Delta for i=p: -(1-h_j) for j≠p. j=p: +h_i for i≠p.
If old=0,new=1: +(1-h_j) for j≠p; -h_i for i≠p.
So need exclude self and use current other bits (which for j=p in first? j=p means i=p self excluded; for first j≠p okay; for second i≠p). Thus A vector for first should have (1-h_q) for q≠p, and 0 at q=p. B vector should have h_i for i≠p, 0 at i=p.

For b=[1,0], p=1 0→1:
A: q=0 val1-b0=0 at k=-1; q=1 val but exclude self 0.
B: i=0 val b0=1 at k=1; i=1 exclude. Delta A-B: k=-1 0, k=1 -1. Original c [0,0,1] -> new [0,0,0]. good.

def swap_delta(b, i, j, L, n):
    delta = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.int16? maybe int)
    one_minus = 1 - b  # uint8
    # flip i 1->0: signA=-1, signB=+1
    start = n-1 - i
    delta[start:start+n] += - one_minus  # vector
    # B: startB = n-1+i; slice [startB-n+1 : startB+1]
    st = n-1 + i
    delta[st-n+1:st+1] += b[::-1]
    delta[n-1] -= 1  # s_self for old1? Let's compute general formula maybe.
```

```
h=np.clip(np.asarray(h,float),0,1)
target=n/2
for _ in range(200):
 diff=target-h.sum()
 if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
 if diff>0:
   room=1-h; tot=room.sum()
   if tot<1e-15: break
   # add proportional to room
   h += diff/tot*room
 else:
   tot=h.sum()
   if tot<1e-15: break
   h += diff/tot*h
return np.clip(h,0,1)
```

Let's flesh binary local search more.

`binary_local_search(h_init, n, deadline, target_T=None, max_iters?)`:
- Convert to binary as described. Need h_init maybe length n. If h_init None generate random balanced.
- Ensure n even. If not, n=n+1? But target sum? We'll call with even.
- `b` uint8, `c` int array from correlate. Since 1-b uint8, correlate returns int64? We can cast to int32 for speed? `c = np.correlate(b, 1-b, mode='full').astype(np.int32)`. For n up to 2000, max 2000 fits int32.
- T = int(c.max()). if T<=target_T return.
- Maintaining `ones_list` and `zeros_list` maybe as np arrays. After accept, update: if i removed from ones, add to zeros, etc. Easier recompute `ones_idx=np.nonzero(b)[0]`, `zeros_idx=np.nonzero(1-b)[0]` after accept; O(n). Accept maybe many, okay. For random fast use `np.random.choice(ones_idx, size=...)`.
- Active positions for candidates: function `sample_active_candidates(b,c,n,k_active=3, sample_size=12, rng)`:
   Return ones_cand, zeros_cand arrays.
   Determine worst indices: `worst = np.where(c >= c.max() - 1)`? Counts integer; shifts with max. If many > max? maybe all. Take top by c: `ord = np.argsort(-c[:])` no copy? L~2000, sorting each iteration okay but maybe. Better `active = np.where(c == T)[0]`; if len>10, random subset.
   For each active r, k=r-(n-1). For k>=0:
      nv=n-k
      mask = b[0:nv] & (1-b[k:n])  # ones at i, zeros at j
      idx = np.nonzero(mask)[0]
      if idx.size:
         # sample up to sample_size ones positions i
         choose = rng.choice(idx, size=min(sample_size, idx.size), replace=False)
         ones_cand.extend(i[choose]); zeros_cand.extend(i[choose]+k)
   For k<0: kk=-k, nv=n-kk. Pairs i=kk..n-1, j=i-kk. We can reindex a=0..nv-1, i=a+kk, j=a. mask = b[kk:n] & (1-b[0:nv]). ones positions i=a+kk; zeros positions j=a.
   If no active candidates, use random.
- Generate candidate pairs: cross product of sample ones and zeros, but avoid i==j. Also prioritize ones/zeros from active. Use a set? We'll create list maybe up to 200.
- Batch search:
```
no_improve=0
temp=3.0
for it in range(max_iter):
  if time deadline break
  active_ones, active_zeros = sample...
  if len active < 1: random 40 ones/zeros
  candidates = cross + random pairs
  if len(candidates) > 300: random sample 300
  best_candidate=None; best_score=(T, sum_active?); best_delta=None; best_newc=None
  for i,j in candidates:
     if i==j or b[i]!=1 or b[j]!=0: continue
     delta=swap_delta(...)
     newc=c+delta
     Tnew=int(newc.max())
     # score = Tnew, and maybe sum of top values
     if Tnew < best_score[0] or (tie and sumtop lower): store
  apply best if Tnew < T or (SA and accept)
  if Tnew < T: no_improve=0; temp*=0.95 ...
  if SA: accept if Tnew<=target_T or (Tnew-T >0 and rng.random()<exp(-diff/temp)) if T>target maybe.
  if no_improve>50: perturb: do 20 random swaps (not checking aim?) update c; maybe restart from saved best with more temp.
```

Initial binary construction from h:
```
def to_binary_h(h,n):
 target=n//2
 if n%2: ... not use
 b=(h>=0.5).astype(np.uint8)
 s=b.sum()
 if s>target:
    # turn ones with highest h to zero
    ones=np.where(b==1)[0]
    order=ones[np.argsort(h[ones])]  # lowest h first
    for idx in order:
      b[idx]=0; s-=1
      if s==target: break
 elif s<target:
    zeros=np.where(b==0)[0]
    order=zeros[np.argsort(-h[zeros])] # highest h first
    for idx in order: b[idx]=1; s+=1; if s==target break
 return b
```

Implement base per swap directly:
```
delta = zeros(L)
# i 1->0
stA = nminus1 - i
delta[stA:stA+n] -= om_i  # omit self correction later
stB = nminus1 + i
delta[stB-n+1:stB+1] += b_i_rev
delta[nminus1] -= 1
# j 0->1
stA = nminus1 - j
delta[stA:stA+n] += om_i
stB = nminus1 + j
delta[stB-n+1:stB+1] -= b_i_rev
delta[nminus1] -= 1
# cross
r1 = n-1 + (j - i)
r2 = n-1 + (i - j)
delta[r1] += 1
delta[r2] += 1
```

Now continuous: Maybe if binary can find target, we can skip long continuous. But initial binary from fractional may become stuck. We can incorporate simulated annealing restarts.

Let's write binary function robust:
```
def binary_search_from_h(hfr, n, deadline, target_T, rng):
    b = make_binary(...)
    return binary_optimize(b,n,deadline,target_T,rng, init_h=hfr maybe for thresholds)
```
`binary_optimize`:
- Precompute arrays.
- best_b copy, best_T.
- If T<=target return best.
- current b copy.
- no_improve=0; temp=4.0
- while time < deadline-1:
   - if T <= target return.
   - if no_improve > 200: perturb current: perform `perturbations=50` random swaps with no improvement? But need maintain c. Could just restart from best_b with temp *=2? Starting fresh maybe better. Implement restart_count.

Detailed helper implementations:

`compute_c5_vec(h,n)`:
```
def compute_c5_vec(h,n):
    dx=2.0/n
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')*dx
```
`compute_c5`:
```
def compute_c5(h,n):
    return float(np.max(compute_c5_vec(h,n)))
```
`project_feasible(h,n)`:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    h=np.clip(np.asarray(h,dtype=float),0.0,1.0)
    target=n/2.0
    for _ in range(1000):
        s=h.sum(); diff=target-s
        if abs(diff)<1e-14: break
        if diff>0:
            room=1.0-h; tot=room.sum()
            if tot<1e-15: break
            h += (diff/tot)*room
        else:
            tot=h.sum()
            if tot<1e-15: break
            h += (diff/tot)*h
    h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    # final exact adjustment
    for _ in range(10):
        diff=target-h.sum()
        if abs(diff)<1e-15: break
        if diff>0:
            idx=int(np.argmax(1.0-h)); h[idx]=min(1.0, h[idx]+diff)
        else:
            idx=int(np.argmax(h)); h[idx]=max(0.0, h[idx]+diff) # diff negative
    return h
```
Final single adjustment may leave sum diff if diff too large (should small). If diff remains, loop. If diff large due many clipped, previous iterative handles.

`symmetrize_project(h,n)`:
```
def symmetrize_project(h,n):
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float)
    h=0.5*(h+h[::-1])
    return project_feasible(h,n)
```

`shift_grad_single` as previous but no dx? Previous included dx. For SQP, c_vec includes dx, so grad should be derivative of c_vec wrt h includes dx. Good.
```
def shift_grad_single(h,n,shift_idx):
    dx=2.0/n; grad=np.zeros(n); k=shift_idx-(n-1)
    if k>=0:
       nv=n-k; grad[0:nv] += (1-h[k:n])*dx
       if nv>0: grad[k:n] += (-h[0:nv])*dx
    else:
       kk=-k; nv=n-kk; grad[kk:n] += (1-h[0:nv])*dx
       if nv>0: grad[0:nv] += (-h[kk:n])*dx
    return grad
```
Check for k<0: Example n=3, k=-1, pair i=1,2 with j=0,1. c=∑_{i=1}^{2}h_i(1-h_{i-1}). Derivative wrt h_1 (i=1 first and i? second for i=2? h_1 is second in pair i=2? c term h2(1-h1); derivative = from first pair h1(1-h0) + from second -h2. Our formula `grad[kk:n]` (1 from kk=1 to2) += (1-h[0:nv])=> grad1 +=(1-h0), grad2 +=(1-h1); `grad[0:nv]` += (-h[kk:n])=> grad0 += -h1, grad1 += -h2. So grad1=(1-h0)-h2, grad2=(1-h1). good.

`smooth_obj`:
```
def softmax_logsumexp(vals): ... no closure
def smooth_obj(x,n,alpha,mu):
    c = compute_c5_vec(x,n)
    m=float(c.max())
    sh=alpha*(c-m)
    mx=sh.max()
    val = m + (mx + np.log(np.sum(np.exp(sh-mx))))/alpha
    val += 0.5*mu*(float(x.sum())-n/2.0)**2
    return float(val)
```
If alpha large and sh range >700, use mx shift already; exp underflow ok. Use if mx>500? Not needed with shift.
`smooth_grad`:
```
def smooth_grad(x,n,alpha,mu):
   c=compute_c5_vec(x,n); m=c.max(); sh=alpha*(c-m); mx=sh.max(); w=np.exp(sh-mx); w/=w.sum(); dx=2/n
   grad=zeros
   for mm in range(2n-1): if w[mm]>1e-14: wm=w[mm]*dx; k=mm-(n-1); ... same as shift_grad but multiply wm (without dx? shift_grad_single includes dx; multiply wm? shift_grad_single returns ∂c_m/∂h including dx. For weighted sum of c: Σ w_m c_m; gradient = Σ w_m grad_m. So call shift_grad_single and add w[mm]*grad. Or inline with wm= w[mm] (no dx) and use dx in formula. Use shift_grad_single for simplicity but loops. 2n ~1000, active weights maybe all. Fine.
   for mm:
      if w[mm]>1e-14: grad += w[mm] * shift_grad_single(x,n,mm)
   grad += mu*(x.sum()-n/2.0)*np.ones(n)
   return grad
```

for mm in idx: grad += w[mm]*shift_grad_single(...)
```

`sqp_lp_step(h,n,active_idx,active_vals,grads,rho)` maybe compute inside to avoid recompute. We'll implement in `sqp_optimize` directly to build arrays.
```
def sqp_solve_step(h,n,active_idx,rho):
   c_vec=compute_c5_vec(h,n)
   n_act=len(active_idx); c_obj=zeros(n+1); c_obj[-1]=1
   A_ub=zeros((n_act,n+1)); b_ub=zeros(n_act)
   for j,idx in enumerate(active_idx):
      g=shift_grad_single(h,n,idx)
      A_ub[j,0:n]=g
      b_ub[j]=-float(c_vec[idx])
   A_eq=zeros((1,n)); A_eq[0,:]=1
   b_eq=[0.0]
   bounds=[]
   for i in range(n):
      lo=max(-rho, -h[i])
      hi=min(rho, 1.0-h[i])
      if lo>hi: lo=hi=0
      bounds.append((lo,hi))
   bounds.append((0.0,2.0))
   try: res=linprog(c_obj,A_ub=A_ub,b_ub=b_ub,A_eq=A_eq,b_eq=b_eq,bounds=bounds,method='highs', options={'disp':False})
   except: return None
   if not res.success or res.x is None: return None
   return np.asarray(res.x[:n],float)
```
Need A_eq shape (1,n) and b_eq length1.
Potential numerical: c_vec active constraints and grad; if c_vec exact, active constraints require gδ - t ≤ -c. For small δ, active c predicted c+gδ; t. At δ=0,t=c satisfies equality. LP min t. Good.
If `res.status` maybe 0. Use success.

`sqp_optimize`:
```
def sqp_optimize(h,n,deadline,max_iter=300,rho=0.04,sym=False):
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    if sym: h=symmetrize_project(h,n)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=compute_c5(h,n)
    local_h=h
    no=0
    for it in range(max_iter):
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       c_vec=compute_c5_vec(local_h,n)
       mv=float(c_vec.max())
       tol=max(1e-10, 1e-4*mv)
       active=np.where(c_vec >= mv - tol)[0]
       if active.size==0: break
       if active.size>25: active=active[np.argsort(-c_vec[active])[:25]]
       active=list(active)
       delta=sqp_solve_step(local_h,n,active,rho)
       if delta is None:
           rho*=0.5
           if rho<1e-8: break
           continue
       if np.max(np.abs(delta))<1e-13: break
       accepted=False
       for step in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125,0.0625):
           cand = local_h + step*delta
           cand=project_feasible(cand,n)
           if sym: cand=symmetrize_project(cand,n)
           c_new=compute_c5(cand,n)
           if c_new < best_c - 1e-14:
               best_c=c_new; best_h=cand.copy(); local_h=cand; no=0; accepted=True
               if rho<0.08: rho=min(rho*1.25,0.1)
               break
       if not accepted:
           no+=1; rho*=0.75
           if rho<1e-9: break
           if no>30: break
    return best_h,best_c
```
Should we update local_h to best_h if not accepted? no. If best_c improved earlier but local at max maybe not improve further. Good.

def lbfgs_optimize(h,n,deadline,configs):
    h=project_feasible(h,n); best_h=h.copy(); best_c=compute_c5(h,n)
    bounds=[(0,1)]*n
    for alpha,mu in configs:
       if time.time()>deadline-10: break
       # maybe sym? no
       try:
          res=minimize(smooth_obj,h,args=(n,alpha,mu),method='L-BFGS-B',bounds=bounds,jac=smooth_grad,options={'maxiter':500,'ftol':1e-14,'gtol':1e-12,'maxfun':10000})
          x=res.x; h_new=project_feasible(x,n); c_new=compute_c5(h_new,n)
          if c_new<best_c:
             best_c=c_new; h_new=sym? optional? If sym? We'll call separate. update h_new
       except: pass
    return best_h,best_c
```

Binary helpers:
`make_binary_from_h(h,n,threshold)`:
```
def make_binary_from_h(h,n,threshold=0.5):
    target=n//2
    b=(h>=threshold).astype(np.uint8)
    s=int(b.sum())
    if s>target:
       ones=np.nonzero(b==1)[0]
       order=ones[np.argsort(h[ones])]
       k=0
       while s>target and k<ones.size:
          idx=order[k]; b[idx]=0; s-=1; k+=1
    elif s<target:
       zeros=np.nonzero(b==0)[0]
       order=zeros[np.argsort(-h[zeros])]
       k=0
       while s<target and k<zeros.size:
          idx=order[k]; b[idx]=1; s+=1; k+=1
    return b
```
If n odd? We only use even. If sum off because target not integer? n even.

def best_binary_initial(h,n):
    best_b=None; best_c=None
    for th in (0.5,0.45,0.55,0.4,0.6,0.35,0.65):
        b=make_binary_from_h(h,n,th)
        c=np.correlate(b,1-b,mode='full').astype(np.int32)
        mc=int(c.max())
        if best_c is None or mc<best_c: best_b=b.copy(); best_c=mc; best_cc=c.copy()
    return best_b,best_cc
```

`binary_swap_delta(b_i, om_i, b_rev_i, n, i, j)` top-level:
```
def binary_swap_delta(b_i, om_i, b_rev_i, n, i, j):
    L=2*n-1; dp1=n-1
    delta=np.zeros(L, dtype=np.int32)
    # i 1->0
    st = dp1 - i
    delta[st:st+n] -= om_i
    st2 = dp1 + i
    delta[st2-n+1:st2+1] += b_rev_i
    delta[dp1] -= 1
    # j 0->1
    st = dp1 - j
    delta[st:st+n] += om_i
    st2 = dp1 + j
    delta[st2-n+1:st2+1] -= b_rev_i
    delta[dp1] -= 1
    r1 = dp1 + (j - i)
    r2 = dp1 + (i - j)
    delta[r1] += 1
    delta[r2] += 1
    return delta
```
Need ensure om_i int16 but del sign: `delta -= om_i` works. If `om_i` is int16, okay. If b_i_rev int16.
Potential: If i/j adjacent? r1/r2 within 0..L yes. If i<j, r1 >dp1, r2<dp1. Both unique. Add +1. If i/j same (not called) r1=r2=dp1; but not.

Test with delta previous examples works.

`binary_apply_swap(b,c,b_i,om_i,b_rev_i,n,i,j,delta=None)`: returns updated c,b arrays and arrays. If delta None compute. We'll c = c + delta; b[i]=0;b[j]=1. Recompute b_i, om_i,b_rev_i. Could do:

b[i]=0; b[j]=1

```

`binary_generate_candidates(b, c, n, rng, max_cands=150)`:
Top-level. Need active T. Use b uint8.
Implementation careful with RandomState: `rng.randint(0, size)` if size>0.
```
def binary_generate_candidates(b,c,n,rng,max_cands=150):
    L=len(c); T=int(c.max())
    active=np.where(c==T)[0]
    if active.size>6:
       active=rng.choice(active, size=6, replace=False)
    ones_sets=[]; zeros_sets=[]
    for r in active:
       k=r-(n-1)
       if k>=0:
          nv=n-k
          if nv<=0: continue
          mask = b[0:nv] & (1-b[k:n])
          idx=np.nonzero(mask)[0]
          if idx.size:
             s=8 if idx.size>=8 else int(idx.size)
             ch=rng.choice(idx, size=s, replace=False)
             ones_sets.extend(ch.tolist())
             zeros_sets.extend((ch+k).tolist())
       else:
          kk=-k; nv=n-kk
          if nv<=0: continue
          mask = b[kk:n] & (1-b[0:nv])
          idx=np.nonzero(mask)[0]
          if idx.size:
             s=8 if idx.size>=8 else int(idx.size)
             ch=rng.choice(idx, size=s, replace=False)
             ones_sets.extend((ch+kk).tolist())
             zeros_sets.extend(ch.tolist())
    cands=[]
    # use set to dedup active
    ones_u = list(set(ones_sets)) if ones_sets else []
    zeros_u = list(set(zeros_sets)) if zeros_sets else []
    if len(ones_u) and len(zeros_u):
       if len(ones_u)>12: ones_u = list(rng.choice(ones_u,size=12,replace=False))
       if len(zeros_u)>12: zeros_u = list(rng.choice(zeros_u,size=12,replace=False))
       for i in ones_u:
          for j in zeros_u:
             if i != j: cands.append((int(i),int(j)))
    # random candidates
    ones_all=np.nonzero(b)[0]; zeros_all=np.nonzero(1-b)[0]
    nrw = max(20, max_cands - len(cands))
    for _ in range(min(nrw, 80)):
       i=int(ones_all[rng.randint(0,len(ones_all))]); j=int(zeros_all[rng.randint(0,len(zeros_all))])
       if i!=j: cands.append((i,j))
    # limit
    if len(cands)>max_cands:
       idx=rng.choice(len(cands), size=max_cands, replace=False)
       cands=[cands[int(x)] for x in idx]
    # if empty, choose random if exist
    if not cands and ones_all.size and zeros_all.size:
       for i in range(10):
          ii=int(ones_all[rng.randint(0,len(ones_all))]); jj=int(zeros_all[rng.randint(0,len(zeros_all))])
          if ii!=jj: cands.append((ii,jj))
    return cands
```
Could be slow using sets each batch; small. fine.

Now, code final. Need ensure no undefined variables. We'll write in final answer only code. But before final, let's mentally review binary delta with cross corrections with simultaneous flips in all cases.

Full delta for swap i=1->0, j=0->1. For each pair (u,v):
- u=i: delta = (1 - old_other) * (new_val - old_val) for v≠i = -(1-b_v). If v=j, old b_j=0 => -1; individual A_i gives -1, base_j B_j gives? B_j includes u? B_j is j as second? Term u=i,v=j: i first/j second; base_j second (v=j) old h_i=1 sign - => -1. Sum -2, cross +1 => -1 good.
- u=j: delta old=0,new=1: +(1-b_v) for v≠j. If v=i: +(1-b_i)=0; base_i A_i? A_i i as first no; base\_i B_i? u? B_i second v? For term u=j,v=i: j first/i second; base_i second? v=i, sign + adds old h_u (j) =0; base_j first adds (1-b_i)=0; sum0, cross +1 => +1 good.
- v=i: delta u≠i: old h_u*(0), new h_u*(1) => +h_u. If u=j: old0 =>0; base_i second adds old h_u=0; base_j? v=j second? u? no; sum0, cross? term covered. If u other: base_i second adds h_u correct.
- v=j: delta u≠j: old h_u*(1), new h_u*0 => -h_u. If u=i: old1 => -1; base_i first? u=i first? v=j handled cross; base_j second sign - adds -old h_i = -1; base_i first? u=i first, v=j A_i -1; sum -2, cross +1. If u other: base_j second -h_u correct.
- self terms: u=v=i: old1*0=0,new0*1=0 delta0. Base_i first - (1-b_i)=0? b_i=1 so -0; second +b_i=1; corr -1 ->0. Base_j no. Self j: base_j first + (1-b_j)=1; second -b_j=0; corr -1 ->0. Good. ```
def lbfgs_optimize(h,n,deadline,configs=None,sym=False):
   h=project_feasible(h,n)
   if sym: h=symmetrize_project(h,n)
   best_h=h.copy(); best_c=compute_c5(h,n)
   bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
   if configs is None: configs=[(1000,100.0),(5000,500.0),(20000,5000.0)]
   for alpha,mu in configs:
      if time.time() > deadline - 10: break
      try:
         res = minimize(smooth_obj, h, args=(n, alpha, mu), method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, jac=smooth_grad, options={'maxiter':300, 'ftol':1e-14, 'gtol':1e-12, 'maxfun':5000})
         x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
         x = project_feasible(x,n)
         if sym: x = symmetrize_project(x,n)
         c_new=compute_c5(x,n)
         if c_new < best_c:
            best_c=c_new; best_h=x.copy(); h=x
      except Exception: pass
   return best_h,best_c
```

```python
import numpy as np
import math
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize, linprog

TARGET_C = 0.3808


def compute_c5_vec(h, n):
    dx = 2.0 / n
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full") * dx


def compute_c5(h, n):
    return float(np.max(compute_c5_vec(h, n)))


def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.clip(np.asarray(h, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0)
    target = n / 2.0
    for _ in range(1000):
        s = float(h.sum())
        diff = target - s
        if abs(diff) < 1e-14:
            break
        if diff > 0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            tot = float(room.sum())
            if tot < 1e-15:
                break
            h += (diff / tot) * room
        else:
            tot = float(h.sum())
            if tot < 1e-15:
                break
            h += (diff / tot) * h
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)

    for _ in range(20):
        diff = target - float(h.sum())
        if abs(diff) < 1e-15:
            break
        if diff > 0:
            idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h))
            h[idx] = min(1.0, h[idx] + diff)
        else:
            idx = int(np.argmax(h))
            h[idx] = max(0.0, h[idx] + diff)
    return h


def symmetrize_project(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    h = 0.5 * (h + h[::-1])
    return project_feasible(h, n)


def resample_h(h, n_new):
    if len(h) == n_new:
        return np.array(h, dtype=float, copy=True)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, len(h), endpoint=False)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n_new, endpoint=False)
    return np.interp(x_new, x_old, h)


def shift_grad_single(h, n, shift_idx):
    dx = 2.0 / n
    grad = np.zeros(n)
    k = shift_idx - (n - 1)
    if k >= 0:
        nv = n - k
        if nv > 0:
            grad[0:nv] += (1.0 - h[k:n]) * dx
            grad[k:n] += (-h[0:nv]) * dx
    else:
        kk = -k
        nv = n - kk
        if nv > 0:
            grad[kk:n] += (1.0 - h[0:nv]) * dx
            grad[0:nv] += (-h[kk:n]) * dx
    return grad


def smooth_obj(x, n, alpha, mu):
    c = compute_c5_vec(x, n)
    m = float(c.max())
    sh = alpha * (c - m)
    mx = float(sh.max())
    val = m + (mx + float(np.log(np.sum(np.exp(sh - mx))))) / alpha
    diff = float(x.sum()) - n / 2.0
    val += 0.5 * mu * diff * diff
    return float(val)


def smooth_grad(x, n, alpha, mu):
    c = compute_c5_vec(x, n)
    m = float(c.max())
    sh = alpha * (c - m)
    mx = float(sh.max())
    w = np.exp(sh - mx)
    w /= w.sum()
    idx = np.where(w > 1e-10)[0]
    if idx.size == 0:
        idx = np.array([int(np.argmax(w))])
    if idx.size > 40:
        order = np.argsort(-w[idx])
        idx = idx[order[:40]]
    grad = np.zeros(n)
    for mm in idx:
        if float(w[mm]) > 1e-14:
            grad += float(w[mm]) * shift_grad_single(x, n, int(mm))
    diff = float(x.sum()) - n / 2.0
    grad += mu * diff * np.ones(n)
    return grad


def sqp_solve_step(h, n, active_idx, rho):
    c_vec = compute_c5_vec(h, n)
    n_act = len(active_idx)
    c_obj = np.zeros(n + 1)
    c_obj[-1] = 1.0
    A_ub = np.zeros((n_act, n + 1))
    b_ub = np.zeros(n_act)
    for jj in range(n_act):
        idx = int(active_idx[jj])
        A_ub[jj, 0:n] = shift_grad_single(h, n, idx)
        b_ub[jj] = -float(c_vec[idx])
    A_eq = np.zeros((1, n))
    A_eq[0, 0:n] = 1.0
    b_eq = np.zeros(1)
    bounds = []
    for i in range(n):
        lo = max(-rho, -float(h[i]))
        hi = min(rho, float(1.0 - h[i]))
        if lo > hi:
            lo = hi = 0.0
        bounds.append((lo, hi))
    bounds.append((0.0, 2.0))
    try:
        res = linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=b_ub, A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=b_eq,
                      bounds=bounds, method="highs",
                      options={"disp": False})
    except Exception:
        return None
    if res is None or not res.success or res.x is None:
        return None
    return np.asarray(res.x[:n], dtype=float)


def sqp_optimize(h, n, deadline, max_iter=300, rho_init=0.04, sym=False):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    if sym:
        h = symmetrize_project(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = compute_c5(h, n)
    local_h = h.copy()
    rho = float(rho_init)
    no_improve = 0

    for _ in range(max_iter):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        c_vec = compute_c5_vec(local_h, n)
        mv = float(c_vec.max())
        tol = max(1e-10, 2e-4 * mv)
        active = np.where(c_vec >= mv - tol)[0]
        if active.size == 0:
            break
        if active.size > 25:
            order = np.argsort(-c_vec[active])
            active = active[order[:25]]
        active = list(active)

        delta = sqp_solve_step(local_h, n, active, rho)
        if delta is None:
            rho *= 0.5
            if rho < 1e-8:
                break
            continue
        if float(np.max(np.abs(delta))) < 1e-13:
            break

        accepted = False
        for step in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125, 0.0625):
            cand = local_h + step * delta
            cand = project_feasible(cand, n)
            if sym:
                cand = symmetrize_project(cand, n)
            c_new = compute_c5(cand, n)
            if c_new < best_c - 1e-14:
                best_c = c_new
                best_h = cand.copy()
                local_h = cand.copy()
                no_improve = 0
                accepted = True
                if rho < 0.08:
                    rho = min(rho * 1.25, 0.1)
                break

        if not accepted:
            no_improve += 1
            rho *= 0.75
            if rho < 1e-9:
                break
            if no_improve > 35:
                break

    return best_h, best_c


def lbfgs_optimize(h, n, deadline, configs=None, sym=False):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    if sym:
        h = symmetrize_project(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = compute_c5(h, n)
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    if configs is None:
        configs = [(800.0, 80.0), (4000.0, 400.0), (16000.0, 4000.0)]

    for alpha, mu in configs:
        if time.time() > deadline - 8.0:
            break
        try:
            res = minimize(
                smooth_obj, h, args=(n, alpha, mu),
                method="L-BFGS-B", jac=smooth_grad, bounds=bounds,
                options={"maxiter": 120, "ftol": 1e-14,
                         "gtol": 1e-12, "maxfun": 2500}
            )
            x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
            x = project_feasible(x, n)
            if sym:
                x = symmetrize_project(x, n)
            c_new = compute_c5(x, n)
            if c_new < best_c:
                best_c = c_new
                best_h = x.copy()
                h = x.copy()
        except Exception:
            pass

    return best_h, best_c


def make_binary_from_h(h, n, threshold=0.5):
    target = n // 2
    b = (h >= threshold).astype(np.uint8)
    s = int(b.sum())
    if s > target:
        ones = np.nonzero(b == 1)[0]
        order = ones[np.argsort(h[ones])]
        k = 0
        while s > target and k < ones.size:
            idx = int(order[k])
            b[idx] = 0
            s -= 1
            k += 1
    elif s < target:
        zeros = np.nonzero(b == 0)[0]
        order = zeros[np.argsort(-h[zeros])]
        k = 0
        while s < target and k < zeros.size:
            idx = int(order[k])
            b[idx] = 1
            s += 1
            k += 1
    return b


def best_binary_initial(h, n):
    best_b = None
    best_c = None
    best_count = None
    for th in (0.5, 0.48, 0.52, 0.45, 0.55, 0.4, 0.6, 0.35, 0.65):
        b = make_binary_from_h(h, n, th)
        c = np.correlate(b, 1 - b, mode="full").astype(np.int32)
        mc = int(c.max())
        if best_count is None or mc < best_count:
            best_count = mc
            best_b = b.copy()
            best_c = c.copy()
    if best_b is None:
        arr = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.uint8)
        arr[:n // 2] = 1
        np.random.shuffle(arr)
        best_b = arr
        best_c = np.correlate(best_b, 1 - best_b, mode="full").astype(np.int32)
    return best_b, best_c


def binary_arrays(b, n):
    b_i = b.astype(np.int16)
    om_i = 1 - b_i
    b_rev_i = b_i[::-1]
    return b_i, om_i, b_rev_i


def binary_swap_delta(b_i, om_i, b_rev_i, n, i, j):
    L = 2 * n - 1
    mid = n - 1
    delta = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.int32)

    # i: 1 -> 0
    st = mid - i
    delta[st:st + n] -= om_i
    st2 = mid + i
    delta[st2 - n + 1:st2 + 1] += b_rev_i
    delta[mid] -= 1

    # j: 0 -> 1
    st = mid - j
    delta[st:st + n] += om_i
    st2 = mid + j
    delta[st2 - n + 1:st2 + 1] -= b_rev_i
    delta[mid] -= 1

    # interaction corrections for the two pairs (i,j) and (j,i)
    delta[mid + (j - i)] += 1
    delta[mid + (i - j)] += 1
    return delta


def binary_apply(b, c, b_i, om_i, b_rev_i, n, i, j):
    delta = binary_swap_delta(b_i, om_i, b_rev_i, n, i, j)
    c = (c + delta).astype(np.int32, copy=False)
    b[i] = 0
    b[j] = 1
    b_i, om_i, b_rev_i = binary_arrays(b, n)
    return b, c, b_i, om_i, b_rev_i


def binary_generate_candidates(b, c, n, rng, max_cands=80):
    T = int(c.max())
    active = np.where(c == T)[0]
    if active.size == 0:
        active = np.array([int(np.argmax(c))])
    if active.size > 5:
        active = rng.choice(active, size=5, replace=False)

    ones_pos = []
    zeros_pos = []

    for r in active:
        rr = int(r)
        k = rr - (n - 1)
        if k >= 0:
            nv = n - k
            if nv <= 0:
                continue
            mask = b[0:nv] & (1 - b[k:n])
            idx = np.nonzero(mask)[0]
            if idx.size:
                s = int(min(8, idx.size))
                ch = rng.choice(idx, size=s, replace=False)
                ones_pos.extend([int(x) for x in ch])
                zeros_pos.extend([int(x) + k for x in ch])
        else:
            kk = -k
            nv = n - kk
            if nv <= 0:
                continue
            mask = b[kk:n] & (1 - b[0:nv])
            idx = np.nonzero(mask)[0]
            if idx.size:
                s = int(min(8, idx.size))
                ch = rng.choice(idx, size=s, replace=False)
                ones_pos.extend([int(x) + kk for x in ch])
                zeros_pos.extend([int(x) for x in ch])

    cands = []

    if ones_pos:
        ones_u = list(set(ones_pos))
        if len(ones_u) > 10:
            arr = np.array(ones_u)
            ones_u = [int(x) for x in rng.choice(arr, size=10, replace=False)]
    else:
        ones_u = []
    if zeros_pos:
        zeros_u = list(set(zeros_pos))
        if len(zeros_u) > 10:
            arr = np.array(zeros_u)
            zeros_u = [int(x) for x in rng.choice(arr, size=10, replace=False)]
    else:
        zeros_u = []

    if ones_u and zeros_u:
        for ii in ones_u:
            for jj in zeros_u:
                if ii != jj:
                    cands.append((ii, jj))

    ones_all = np.nonzero(b)[0]
    zeros_all = np.nonzero(1 - b)[0]
    nrw = max(15, max_cands - len(cands))
    for _ in range(min(nrw, 70)):
        if ones_all.size == 0 or zeros_all.size == 0:
            break
        ii = int(ones_all[rng.randint(0, ones_all.size)])
        jj = int(zeros_all[rng.randint(0, zeros_all.size)])
        if ii != jj:
            cands.append((ii, jj))

    if len(cands) > max_cands:
        idx = rng.choice(len(cands), size=max_cands, replace=False)
        cands = [cands[int(x)] for x in idx]

    if not cands and ones_all.size and zeros_all.size:
        for _ in range(12):
            ii = int(ones_all[rng.randint(0, ones_all.size)])
            jj = int(zeros_all[rng.randint(0, zeros_all.size)])
            if ii != jj:
                cands.append((ii, jj))
    return cands


def binary_optimize(h, n, deadline, rng):
    if n <= 0 or n % 2 != 0:
        return None, None, n
    target_T = int(math.floor(TARGET_C * n / 2.0 + 1e-12))

    b, c = best_binary_initial(h, n)
    T = int(c.max())
    best_T = T
    best_b = b.copy()
    b_i, om_i, b_rev_i = binary_arrays(b, n)

    max_cands = 90 if n <= 600 else 55
    temp = 3.0
    no_improve = 0
    batch = 0

    while time.time() < deadline - 1.0:
        batch += 1
        cands = binary_generate_candidates(b, c, n, rng, max_cands)
        if not cands:
            break

        T = int(c.max())
        active_idx = np.where(c == T)[0]
        if active_idx.size == 0:
            active_idx = np.array([int(np.argmax(c))])
        if active_idx.size > 32:
            active_idx = rng.choice(active_idx, size=32, replace=False)
        active_sum = int(c[active_idx].sum()) if active_idx.size else 0

        best_i = -1
        best_j = -1
        best_new_T = T + 10000
        best_new_sum = 1000000000

        for (ii, jj) in cands:
            if ii == jj or b[ii] != 1 or b[jj] != 0:
                continue
            delta = binary_swap_delta(b_i, om_i, b_rev_i, n, ii, jj)
            newc = c + delta
            T2 = int(newc.max())
            if T2 > best_new_T:
                continue
            s2 = int(newc[active_idx].sum()) if active_idx.size else 0
            if T2 < best_new_T or (T2 == best_new_T and s2 < best_new_sum):
                best_i = int(ii)
                best_j = int(jj)
                best_new_T = T2
                best_new_sum = s2

        if best_i >= 0:
            ii = best_i
            jj = best_j
            if best_new_T < T:
                accept = True
            elif best_new_T == T and active_idx.size and best_new_sum < active_sum:
                accept = True
            else:
                diff = best_new_T - T
                accept = False
                if diff > 0 and temp > 0.05 and diff <= 3:
                    if rng.random() < math.exp(-diff / temp):
                        accept = True

            if accept:
                b, c, b_i, om_i, b_rev_i = binary_apply(
                    b, c, b_i, om_i, b_rev_i, n, ii, jj
                )
                T = int(c.max())
                if T < best_T:
                    best_T = T
                    best_b = b.copy()
                    no_improve = 0
                    temp = max(0.35, temp * 0.97)
                else:
                    no_improve += 1
                    temp = max(0.35, temp * 0.995)
            else:
                no_improve += 1
        else:
            no_improve += 10

        if no_improve > 50:
            for _ in range(25):
                ones_all = np.nonzero(b)[0]
                zeros_all = np.nonzero(1 - b)[0]
                if ones_all.size == 0 or zeros_all.size == 0:
                    break
                ii = int(ones_all[rng.randint(0, ones_all.size)])
                jj = int(zeros_all[rng.randint(0, zeros_all.size)])
                if ii == jj:
                    continue
                b, c, b_i, om_i, b_rev_i = binary_apply(
                    b, c, b_i, om_i, b_rev_i, n, ii, jj
                )
            T = int(c.max())
            if T > best_T + max(5, best_T // 10):
                b = best_b.copy()
                c = np.correlate(b, 1 - b, mode="full").astype(np.int32)
                b_i, om_i, b_rev_i = binary_arrays(b, n)
                T = best_T
                temp = min(8.0, temp * 1.5)
                no_improve = 0

        if batch % 100 == 0:
            c_check = np.correlate(b, 1 - b, mode="full").astype(np.int32)
            if not np.array_equal(c_check, c):
                c = c_check
                T = int(c.max())

    target = n // 2
    s = int(b.sum())
    if s > target:
        ones = np.nonzero(b == 1)[0]
        k = 0
        while s > target and k < ones.size:
            b[int(ones[k])] = 0
            s -= 1
            k += 1
    elif s < target:
        zeros = np.nonzero(b == 0)[0]
        k = 0
        while s < target and k < zeros.size:
            b[int(zeros[k])] = 1
            s += 1
            k += 1

    c = np.correlate(b, 1 - b, mode="full").astype(np.int32)
    T = int(c.max())
    return b.astype(float), float(T * 2.0 / n), n


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    total_budget = max(30.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = t0 + total_budget - 5.0
    if deadline < t0 + 10.0:
        deadline = t0 + 10.0

    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    try:
        h0 = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).ravel().copy()
        n0 = int(len(h0))
    except Exception:
        n0 = 500
        h0 = np.full(n0, 0.5)

    if n0 <= 0:
        n0 = 500
        h0 = np.full(n0, 0.5)

    n0 = int(n0)
    h0 = project_feasible(h0, n0)
    best_h = h0.copy()
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, n0)
    best_n = n0

    if n0 % 2 == 0:
        hsym = symmetrize_project(h0, n0)
        csym = compute_c5(hsym, n0)
        if csym < best_c:
            best_c = csym
            best_h = hsym.copy()
            best_n = n0

    # Continuous optimization on a tractable resolution near the parent.
    n_opt = best_n if 50 <= best_n <= 1000 else 500
    if n_opt % 2 != 0:
        n_opt += 1
    h_opt = resample_h(best_h, n_opt)
    h_opt = project_feasible(h_opt, n_opt)

    h_sym_opt = symmetrize_project(h_opt, n_opt)
    c_sym_opt = compute_c5(h_sym_opt, n_opt)
    c_opt = compute_c5(h_opt, n_opt)
    h_cont = h_sym_opt if c_sym_opt <= c_opt else h_opt.copy()
    if c_cont := (c_sym_opt if c_sym_opt <= c_opt else c_opt) < best_c:
        best_c = c_cont
        best_h = h_cont.copy()
        best_n = n_opt

    t1 = min(time.time() + 130.0, deadline - 520.0)
    if t1 > time.time() + 5.0:
        h1, c1 = sqp_optimize(h_cont, n_opt, t1, max_iter=400,
                              rho_init=0.05, sym=True)
        if c1 < best_c:
            best_c = c1
            best_h = h1.copy()
            best_n = n_opt

    t2 = min(time.time() + 60.0, deadline - 430.0)
    if t2 > time.time() + 5.0:
        h2, c2 = lbfgs_optimize(h_cont, n_opt, t2,
                                configs=[(1200.0, 120.0), (6000.0, 800.0)],
                                sym=True)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_c = c2
            best_h = h2.copy()
            best_n = n_opt

    t3 = min(time.time() + 70.0, deadline - 350.0)
    if t3 > time.time() + 5.0:
        h3, c3 = sqp_optimize(h_cont, n_opt, t3, max_iter=250,
                              rho_init=0.03, sym=False)
        if c3 < best_c:
            best_c = c3
            best_h = h3.copy()
            best_n = n_opt

    # Binary search at 500: if successful this immediately gives C <= 0.38.
    if best_c > 0.38075:
        t4 = min(time.time() + 160.0, deadline - 300.0)
        if t4 > time.time() + 10.0:
            h_bin = resample_h(best_h, 500)
            hb, cb, nb = binary_optimize(h_bin, 500, t4, rng)
            if cb is not None and cb < best_c:
                best_c = cb
                best_h = hb.copy()
                best_n = nb

    # Move to a higher resolution if all that is left can be used productively.
    n_high = 1000
    if time.time() < deadline - 170.0:
        h_high = resample_h(best_h, n_high)
        h_high = project_feasible(h_high, n_high)

        t5 = min(time.time() + 90.0, deadline - 170.0)
        if t5 > time.time() + 8.0:
            h_high_s = symmetrize_project(h_high, n_high)
            hh, ch = sqp_optimize(h_high_s, n_high, t5, max_iter=180,
                                  rho_init=0.04, sym=True)
            if ch < best_c:
                best_c = ch
                best_h = hh.copy()
                best_n = n_high

        t6 = min(time.time() + 40.0, deadline - 120.0)
        if t6 > time.time() + 5.0:
            hh, ch = lbfgs_optimize(best_h if len(best_h) == n_high else resample_h(best_h, n_high),
                                    n_high, t6,
                                    configs=[(4000.0, 400.0), (16000.0, 3000.0)],
                                    sym=True)
            if ch < best_c:
                best_c = ch
                best_h = hh.copy()
                best_n = n_high

    # Final attempt: binary search at high resolution.
    if time.time() < deadline - 20.0:
        h_bin_high = resample_h(best_h, n_high)
        hb, cb, nb = binary_optimize(h_bin_high, n_high, deadline - 2.0, rng)
        if cb is not None and cb < best_c:
            best_c = cb
            best_h = hb.copy()
            best_n = nb

    n_final = int(len(best_h))
    best_h = np.clip(np.asarray(best_h, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0)
    target = n_final / 2.0
    if abs(float(best_h.sum()) - target) > 1e-12:
        best_h = project_feasible(best_h, n_final)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, n_final)

    return best_h, float(best_c), n_final
```
